~~~

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs;

use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\EnsurePermissionsOutcome;

final readonly class EnsurePermissionsResult
{
    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $adminOnly     roles skipped because they are no longer pre-wave-0b pristine
     * @param  list<string>  $missingRoles  --grant-to names with no resolvable role in this tenant
     */
    public function __construct(
        public EnsurePermissionsOutcome $outcome,
        public string $reason,
        public int $created,
        public int $granted,
        public array $adminOnly,
        public array $missingRoles,
    ) {}

    /**
     * The per-tenant marker line, spec 4.3.5, same shape as the sync marker.
     *
     * `mode` is rendered HERE, by the DTO, and is therefore identical on the
     * single-tenant and the fleet path. Rev 1 had the fleet command append it
     * after `reason=` while the single-tenant command never added it at all, so
     * two commands with one contract produced two log shapes and only one of
     * them was greppable for `mode=DRY_RUN` (plan gate r1 minor).
     */
    public function markerLine(string $tenantId, bool $write): string
    {
        return sprintf(
            'PERMISSIONS-ENSURE tenant=%s outcome=%s mode=%s created=%d granted=%d admin_only=%s missing_roles=%s reason=%s',
            $tenantId,
            $this->outcome->value,
            $write ? 'APPLY' : 'DRY_RUN',
            $this->created,
            $this->granted,
            $this->adminOnly === [] ? '-' : implode(',', $this->adminOnly),
            $this->missingRoles === [] ? '-' : implode(',', $this->missingRoles),
            $this->reason,
        );
    }
}
~~~

- [ ] **Step 2 — the runner. This is the per-tenant routine both commands call.**

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\EnsurePermissionsResult;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Authorization\LegacyRoleBaseline;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\EnsurePermissionsOutcome;
use App\Modules\Identity\Infrastructure\Locks\PermissionWriteLock;
use DomainException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Builder;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Collection;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Permission;
use Spatie\Permission\Models\Role;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;
use Throwable;

/**
 * The wave-0b stopgap catalogue writer (spec 4.3.5).
 *
 * It exists because after lane/w-lot-a-1a merges, `tenants:seed
 * RolesAndPermissionsSeeder` writes NOTHING on a marked tenant while
 * LOT_ACTION_PERMISSIONS_ENFORCE is false (its shipped default) — correct for
 * that lane, fatal for a wave that gates live routes on nineteen new keys.
 *
 * permissions:sync SUPERSEDES it entirely in wave 1 (step 2 creates every
 * registry key and step 6 applies template grants, a strict superset), which is
 * why wave 1 deletes this class, both commands and their deploy rows in the
 * same commit that lands permissions:sync.
 */
final readonly class EnsurePermissionsRunner
{
    public function __construct(
        private PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar,
        private PermissionWriteLock $writeLock,
        private LegacyRoleBaseline $baseline,
    ) {}

    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $keys
     * @param  list<string>  $grantTo
     */
    public function run(string $tenantId, array $keys, array $grantTo, bool $write): EnsurePermissionsResult
    {
        // The team boundary lane/w-lot-a-1a ships, adopted verbatim: set before
        // the transaction, restored in a finally that runs on EVERY path.
        $previousTeamId = $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();
        $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($tenantId);

        try {
            return DB::transaction(function () use ($tenantId, $keys, $grantTo, $write): EnsurePermissionsResult {
                // Obligation (ii): the lock is the FIRST statement in the transaction.
                $this->writeLock->acquire($tenantId);

                $created = 0;
                $granted = 0;
                $adminOnly = [];
                $missingRoles = [];

                // Obligation (iii): FOR UPDATE the affected roles, ordered by
                // roles.name ASC then roles.id ASC — the lane's order.
                $wanted = array_values(array_unique(['admin', ...$grantTo]));
                $roles = $this->resolveCatalogueRoles($tenantId, $wanted);

                // 1. Create-only.
                foreach ($keys as $key) {
                    $exists = Permission::query()
                        ->where('name', $key)
                        ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
                        ->exists();
                    if ($exists) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    $created++;
                    if ($write) {
                        Permission::create(['name' => $key, 'guard_name' => 'sanctum']);
                    }
                }

                // 2. admin — unconditional. Restores an invariant, never widens a
                // choice. A tenant with NO resolvable admin is a FAILURE, not a
                // note: its routes are gated on keys nobody holds, and Spatie
                // fails closed, so every caller including the operator running
                // this command gets 403 on twenty-five write routes. Throwing
                // here also rolls the transaction back, which is correct — a run
                // that cannot reach admin has nothing it should have written.
                // (Plan gate r1 B0b-5.)
                $admin = $roles->get('admin');
                if ($admin === null) {
                    throw new AdminRoleMissing($tenantId);
                }
                $granted += $this->grantMissing($admin, $keys, $write);

                // 3. Every other --grant-to role, equality-guarded, snapshot ONCE.
                foreach ($grantTo as $roleName) {
                    if ($roleName === 'admin') {
                        continue;
                    }
                    $role = $roles->get($roleName);
                    if ($role === null) {
                        $missingRoles[] = $roleName;

                        continue;
                    }

                    // Obligation (iv): the eligibility decision is made from the
                    // POST-LOCK read, once per role, BEFORE any of that role's
                    // additions are written. Per-key evaluation would refuse every
                    // key after the first in the same invocation.
                    $current = $this->currentKeys($role);
                    if (! $this->baseline->matchesPreWave0b($roleName, $current)) {
                        $adminOnly[] = $roleName;

                        continue;
                    }

                    $granted += $this->grantMissing($role, $keys, $write);
                }

                $outcome = ($created === 0 && $granted === 0)
                    ? EnsurePermissionsOutcome::AlreadyCurrent
                    : EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Applied;

                $result = new EnsurePermissionsResult(
                    $outcome,
                    $write ? 'ensure_applied' : 'dry_run',
                    $created,
                    $granted,
                    array_values(array_unique($adminOnly)),
                    array_values(array_unique($missingRoles)),
                );

                if (! $write) {
                    // Write-free, not failure-free: the classification above is
                    // identical to an applying run; only the rows are rolled back.
                    throw new DryRunRollback($result);
                }

                return $result;
            });
        } catch (DryRunRollback $rollback) {
            // A dry run is WRITE-free, not FAILURE-free: the sentinel carries the
            // classification out of the rolled-back transaction UNCHANGED, so an
            // admin_role_missing tenant is FAILED on the dry-run path too.
            return $rollback->result;
        } catch (AdminRoleMissing) {
            return new EnsurePermissionsResult(
                EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Failed,
                'admin_role_missing',
                0,
                0,
                [],
                ['admin'],
            );
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {
            return new EnsurePermissionsResult(
                EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Blocked,
                $exception->getMessage(),
                0,
                0,
                [],
                [],
            );
        } catch (Throwable $exception) {
            // EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Failed must be REACHABLE from the runner,
            // or the single-tenant command's promised FAILED marker is a shape no
            // code can produce and a non-DomainException escapes as an uncaught
            // stack trace with no marker at all (plan gate r1 minor). The message
            // is the reason token; the fleet command turns it into a
            // FleetTenantOutcome::failed and a non-zero exit.
            return new EnsurePermissionsResult(
                EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Failed,
                'exception:'.$exception->getMessage(),
                0,
                0,
                [],
                [],
            );
        } finally {
            $this->permissionRegistrar->setPermissionsTeamId($previousTeamId);
            $this->permissionRegistrar->forgetCachedPermissions();
        }
    }

    /**
     * lane/w-lot-a-1a's resolveCatalogueRoles() verbatim, minus the hard-coded
     * general_manager provisioning: NULL-or-tenant team predicate, tenant_id
     * PRESERVED as found, ordered name then id, lockForUpdate, and both refusals.
     *
     * @param  list<string>  $names
     * @return Collection<string, Role>
     */
    private function resolveCatalogueRoles(string $tenantId, array $names): Collection
    {
        $team = config('permission.column_names.team_foreign_key');
        /** @var Collection<int, Role> $roles */
        $roles = Role::query()
            ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
            ->whereIn('name', $names)
            ->where(fn (Builder $query): Builder => $query->whereNull($team)->orWhere($team, $tenantId))
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->orderBy('id')
            ->lockForUpdate()
            ->get();

        foreach ($roles->groupBy('name') as $name => $matches) {
            if ($name === 'general_manager' && $matches->contains(
                static fn (Role $role): bool => $role->getAttribute('provisioning_source') !== 'w-lot-a-1a',
            )) {
                throw new DomainException('unmarked_general_manager_collision');
            }
            if ($matches->count() !== 1) {
                throw new DomainException('ambiguous_legacy_role_collision');
            }
        }

        return $roles->keyBy('name');
    }

    /** @return list<string> */
    private function currentKeys(Role $role): array
    {
        /** @var list<string> $keys */
        $keys = $role->permissions()->toBase()->pluck('name')->all();

        return $keys;
    }

    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $keys
     * @return int the number of grants this call added (or would add, in a dry run)
     */
    private function grantMissing(Role $role, array $keys, bool $write): int
    {
        $existing = $this->currentKeys($role);
        $missing = array_values(array_diff($keys, $existing));
        if ($missing === []) {
            return 0;
        }
        if ($write) {
            // ONE batch per role — the snapshot was taken once, so the batch is
            // what the snapshot approved.
            $role->givePermissionTo($missing);
        }

        return count($missing);
    }
}
~~~

      …and the sentinel it throws, beside it:

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\DTOs\EnsurePermissionsResult;
use RuntimeException;

/**
 * Rolls the per-tenant transaction back while carrying the classification out.
 * A dry run is WRITE-free, not FAILURE-free: the outcome, the counters, the
 * marker and the exit code are identical to an applying run.
 */
final class DryRunRollback extends RuntimeException
{
    public function __construct(public readonly EnsurePermissionsResult $result)
    {
        parent::__construct('dry_run');
    }
}
~~~

      …and the fail-closed sentinel for the missing-admin case, beside them (plan gate r1 **B0b-5**):

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services;

use RuntimeException;

/**
 * The tenant has no resolvable `admin` role, so nothing in it holds the keys its
 * routes are now gated on. Spatie fails closed: that tenant is 403 for EVERY
 * caller on every route this wave gates, including the operator running the
 * deploy.
 *
 * It extends RuntimeException, NOT DomainException, deliberately. DomainException
 * maps to BLOCKED — "this tenant is in a state the command declines to change"
 * — and BLOCKED is a shape an operator may reasonably re-run past. A tenant with
 * no admin is not that: it is a FAILURE, it must exit non-zero on the applying
 * AND the dry-run path, and it must never let a deploy checklist write
 * `permissions_ensure=ok`.
 */
final class AdminRoleMissing extends RuntimeException
{
    public function __construct(public readonly string $tenantId)
    {
        parent::__construct('admin_role_missing');
    }
}
~~~

- [ ] **Step 3 — the two commands.**

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services\EnsurePermissionsRunner;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\EnsurePermissionsOutcome;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;
use Spatie\Permission\PermissionRegistrar;

/**
 * The SINGLE-TENANT operator form, for repairing one tenant. The wave-0b
 * DEPLOY runs permissions:ensure-fleet (spec 4.3.5, rev-8 gate r7 M-1) —
 * `tenants:run permissions:ensure` is never valid, because tenants:run
 * discards child exit statuses and would report a partial deploy as clean.
 */
final class EnsurePermissions extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:ensure
        {--keys=*     : permission keys to create if absent}
        {--grant-to=* : role names that must hold every named key (in addition to admin)}
        {--dry-run    : report the plan; write nothing}';

    protected $description = 'Additively create wave-0b permission keys and grant them where the role is still pristine.';

    public function __construct(
        private readonly EnsurePermissionsRunner $runner,
        private readonly PermissionRegistrar $permissionRegistrar,
    ) {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        $tenantId = $this->currentTenantId();
        if ($tenantId === null) {
            $this->error('permissions:ensure needs a tenant context. Run it under tenancy, or use permissions:ensure-fleet.');

            return self::FAILURE;
        }

        /** @var list<string> $keys */
        $keys = array_values(array_filter((array) $this->option('keys'), 'is_string'));
        /** @var list<string> $grantTo */
        $grantTo = array_values(array_filter((array) $this->option('grant-to'), 'is_string'));

        if ($keys === []) {
            $this->error('permissions:ensure requires at least one --keys= value.');

            return self::FAILURE;
        }

        $write = ! (bool) $this->option('dry-run');
        $result = $this->runner->run($tenantId, $keys, $grantTo, $write);

        $marker = $result->markerLine($tenantId, $write);
        $this->line($marker);
        Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);

        if ($result->outcome === EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Failed && $result->reason === 'admin_role_missing') {
            // Loud, and the same on both paths. A dry run that cannot find admin
            // has found a real defect, not an absence of work.
            $this->error(sprintf(
                'tenant %s has no resolvable `admin` role. Every route this wave gates is 403 for EVERY caller in that '
                .'tenant, admin included, because Spatie fails closed on a key nobody holds. Do NOT record this deploy '
                .'as successful: resolve the missing role first, then re-run.',
                $tenantId,
            ));
        }

        foreach ($result->adminOnly as $role) {
            $this->warn(sprintf(
                'role `%s` has been customised in this tenant — the named keys were granted to `admin` only; '
                .'grant them deliberately in Settings → Roles, or they will arrive with the wave-1 *Re-apply template* action.',
                $role,
            ));
        }
        foreach ($result->missingRoles as $role) {
            $this->warn(sprintf('role `%s` does not exist in this tenant and was skipped; permissions:ensure creates no role.', $role));
        }

        return $result->outcome === EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Blocked || $result->outcome === EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Failed
            ? self::FAILURE
            : self::SUCCESS;
    }

    private function currentTenantId(): ?string
    {
        $id = tenant('id') ?? $this->permissionRegistrar->getPermissionsTeamId();

        return is_string($id) && $id !== '' ? $id : null;
    }
}
~~~

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace App\Console\Commands;

use App\Modules\Identity\Application\Services\EnsurePermissionsRunner;
use App\Modules\Identity\Domain\Enums\EnsurePermissionsOutcome;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Application\DTOs\FleetTenantOutcome;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Application\Services\TenantFleetRunner;
use App\Modules\Tenant\Domain\Tenant;
use Illuminate\Console\Command;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log;

/**
 * The FLEET form the wave-0b deploy runs.
 *
 * --keys and --grant-to are declared on THIS command and handed to the
 * per-tenant routine as PHP arrays, so tenants:run's --option=['k=v'] string
 * encoding never appears and cannot be mistyped into silence.
 *
 * Deleted in wave 1 with EnsurePermissions and EnsurePermissionsRunner.
 */
final class EnsurePermissionsFleet extends Command
{
    protected $signature = 'permissions:ensure-fleet
        {--tenants=*  : limit to these tenant ids or slugs; omit for every tenant}
        {--keys=*     : forwarded verbatim to the per-tenant ensure routine}
        {--grant-to=* : forwarded verbatim to the per-tenant ensure routine}
        {--dry-run    : report the plan for every tenant; write nothing}';

    protected $description = 'Run permissions:ensure across the tenant fleet, with per-tenant isolation and a non-zero exit on any failure.';

    public function __construct(
        private readonly TenantFleetRunner $fleet,
        private readonly EnsurePermissionsRunner $runner,
    ) {
        parent::__construct();
    }

    public function handle(): int
    {
        /** @var list<string> $onlyTenants */
        $onlyTenants = array_values(array_filter((array) $this->option('tenants'), 'is_string'));
        /** @var list<string> $keys */
        $keys = array_values(array_filter((array) $this->option('keys'), 'is_string'));
        /** @var list<string> $grantTo */
        $grantTo = array_values(array_filter((array) $this->option('grant-to'), 'is_string'));
        $write = ! (bool) $this->option('dry-run');

        if ($keys === []) {
            $this->error('permissions:ensure-fleet requires at least one --keys= value.');

            return self::FAILURE;
        }

        $result = $this->fleet->run($onlyTenants, 'PERMISSIONS-ENSURE', function (Tenant $tenant) use ($keys, $grantTo, $write): FleetTenantOutcome {
            $tenantId = (string) $tenant->getAttribute('id');
            $tenantResult = $this->runner->run($tenantId, $keys, $grantTo, $write);

            // `mode=` is rendered by the DTO, so the fleet and single-tenant
            // markers are byte-identical in shape.
            $marker = $tenantResult->markerLine($tenantId, $write);
            $this->line($marker);
            Log::channel('stderr')->info($marker);

            return match ($tenantResult->outcome) {
                EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Applied => FleetTenantOutcome::applied($tenantId, $tenantResult->reason),
                EnsurePermissionsOutcome::AlreadyCurrent => FleetTenantOutcome::alreadyCurrent($tenantId, $tenantResult->reason),
                EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Blocked => FleetTenantOutcome::blocked($tenantId, $tenantResult->reason),
                EnsurePermissionsOutcome::Failed => FleetTenantOutcome::failed($tenantId, $tenantResult->reason),
            };
        });

        $aggregate = $result->aggregateLine('PERMISSIONS-ENSURE');
        $this->line($aggregate);
        Log::channel('stderr')->info($aggregate);

        return $result->isGreen() ? self::SUCCESS : self::FAILURE;
    }
}
~~~

### Steps — part B: wire the lock into every runtime writer (0b-14)

> **The five obligations, per writer:** (i) open a transaction; (ii) `PermissionWriteLock::acquire($tenantId)` as its **first** statement; (iii) `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` the affected `roles` rows ordered by **`roles.name` ASC, then `roles.id` ASC**; (iv) **re-read** the decision inputs after the lock is held and decide from that read; (v) write and commit.
>
> **The one stated exception is `RoleController::store`.** It creates a row that does not yet exist, so there is nothing to `FOR UPDATE` and no prior grant set to re-read. Its obligation is exactly three steps in one transaction: **open the transaction; acquire the lock; INSERT the role, then apply its initial grant set.** A post-insert `FOR UPDATE` on a row the same transaction just created adds **no** serialization beyond the advisory lock it already holds — no other transaction can be between the two statements, because no other transaction holds the lock — so it is not required and **must not be added as ritual**. `PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest` asserts this as a **named property**, not as an exemption from the lock.

- [ ] **Step 4 — `RoleController`, five methods.** Lines re-derived in Phase 0; **re-measured for rev 2 at lane tip `52f5ad796`, where they are unchanged from rev 1** (unlike `UserController`'s — see Step 5): `store` `:209` (signature) / `:213` (`Role::create`) / `:221` (`syncPermissions`), `update` `:239` / `:241` (`findOrFail`) / `:265` (`syncPermissions`), `destroy` `:283` / `:285` / `:312` (`$role->delete()`), `assignRole` `:364` / `:373` (existing `DB::transaction`) / `:385`, `removeRole` `:422` / `:430`.

      Constructor-inject the lock (rule 13 — never `app()`):
      ```php
      public function __construct(
          // …the controller's existing dependencies, unchanged…
          private readonly PermissionWriteLock $permissionWriteLock,
      ) {}
      ```

      **`store`** — the create-path exception:
      ```php
      $role = DB::transaction(function () use ($validated, $tenantId): Role {
          $this->permissionWriteLock->acquire($tenantId);
          $role = Role::create([/* …exactly the existing payload… */]);
          if (array_key_exists('permissions', $validated)) {
              $role->syncPermissions($validated['permissions']);
          }

          return $role;
      });
      ```
      **The other four are written out below rather than left as `/* … */` and a generic `$id`** (plan gate r1 **M0b-3**): they have four different shapes — two take an `int $id`, two take a `string $userId` and resolve a **User**, and only two of the four need a grant-set re-read — so one template could not implement them and reading one would have been guesswork at execution time.

      **`update(UpdateRoleRequest $request, int $id)`** — lane `:239` (signature), `:241` (`findOrFail`), `:265` (`syncPermissions`). The `findOrFail` at `:241` becomes the locked read, and it is the read the decision is made from:
      ```php
      return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $id, $tenantId): JsonResponse {
          $this->permissionWriteLock->acquire($tenantId);
          $role = Role::query()
              ->where('id', $id)
              ->orderBy('name')->orderBy('id')
              ->lockForUpdate()
              ->firstOrFail();
          $validated = $request->validated();
          // …the existing system-role / name-collision checks, UNCHANGED, but now
          // evaluated against $role as re-read under the lock…
          if (array_key_exists('permissions', $validated)) {
              $role->syncPermissions($validated['permissions']);
          }

          return /* …the existing response builder, byte-identical… */;
      });
      ```

      **`destroy(DeleteRoleRequest $request, int $id)`** — lane `:283` (signature), `:285` (resolution), `:312` (`$role->delete()`). `delete()` is not a Spatie write call, which is why the AST census does not enumerate it; it is in `LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS` anyway, because the cascade removes `role_has_permissions` rows and must be serialized against a concurrent grant:
      ```php
      return DB::transaction(function () use ($id, $tenantId): JsonResponse {
          $this->permissionWriteLock->acquire($tenantId);
          $role = Role::query()
              ->where('id', $id)
              ->orderBy('name')->orderBy('id')
              ->lockForUpdate()
              ->firstOrFail();
          // …the existing system-role refusal and in-use check, re-evaluated HERE…
          $role->delete();

          return /* …existing response… */;
      });
      ```

      **`assignRole(AssignRoleRequest $request, string $userId)`** — lane `:364` (signature), `:373` (an existing `DB::transaction`), `:385` (`assignRole`). The transaction already exists, so the change is **one inserted line plus a moved read**: the lock becomes the first statement inside the existing closure, and the **admin-population count moves inside it**. That count becomes the wave-2a last-admin floor's input; 0b only moves *where* it is taken, and changes nothing about what it decides:
      ```php
      return DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $userId, $validated, $actor): JsonResponse {
          $this->permissionWriteLock->acquire($tenantId);
          $role = Role::query()
              ->where('name', $roleName)
              ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
              ->orderBy('name')->orderBy('id')
              ->lockForUpdate()
              ->firstOrFail();
          $user = /* …the existing user resolution, unchanged… */;
          // …the admin population count MOVES HERE, after the lock…
          $user->assignRole($roleName);

          return /* …existing response and audit dispatch, unchanged… */;
      });
      ```

      **`removeRole(AssignRoleRequest $request, string $userId)`** — lane `:422` (signature), `:430` (`removeRole`). This method has **no** transaction today; it gains one with the same shape as `assignRole`'s, and the same moved admin count.

      **Do not change any response body, status code, validation rule, `CrossTenantRoute` annotation or audit dispatch in this task.** The only change is the transaction, the lock, the `FOR UPDATE` and the moved read. The `#[CrossTenantRoute]` attribute on `store` (lane `:208`) stays exactly where it is.

- [ ] **Step 5 — `UserController`, two methods. The rev-1 line numbers were STALE and are corrected here** (plan gate r1 citation audit; re-measured at `lane/w-lot-a-1a` `52f5ad796`). Rev 1 said the transactions were at `:217`/`:357` and the writes at `:234`/`:384`; at the current lane tip they are:

      | Method | Signature | Existing `DB::transaction` | Write |
      |---|---:|---:|---:|
      | `store(CreateUserRequest $request)` | `:197` | **`:225`** | **`:268`** (`assignRole`) |
      | `update(UpdateUserRequest $request, string $id)` | `:318` | **`:375`** | **`:409`** (`syncRoles`) |

      Both already open a transaction, so the lock becomes the **first statement inside the existing closure** and the role resolution moves inside it. Neither method needs a grant-set re-read — they assign a role to a **user**, they do not change a role's grants — so obligation (iv) is satisfied by re-reading the **role** under the lock, which is what the `FOR UPDATE` above already does. **Three other `DB::transaction` blocks in this file (`:501`, `:579`, `:661`) are not role writers and are not touched.**

- [ ] **Step 6 — `ResetTenantCommand`.** Spec §4.3.2's rev-8 M-2 ruling moves it from INITIALIZATION-ONLY to **LOCKED**. The command refuses only in production (`:29-35`), `--force` skips the confirmation (`:59-63`), and it drops and recreates the live schema with no maintenance mode (`:65-71`) — staging and development tenants routinely have active operators, so "no operator can be concurrent" was simply false for this row.

      The correction is the small one: the `assignRole('admin')` loop at `:89-95` moves inside a transaction whose first statement is the lock, taken **after** the schema recreate and migrations:
      ```php
      $users = User::where('tenant_id', $tenant->id)->get();
      setPermissionsTeamId($tenant->id);
      DB::transaction(function () use ($tenant, $users): void {
          $this->permissionWriteLock->acquire($tenant->id);
          $admin = Role::query()
              ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
              ->where('name', 'admin')
              ->where(fn (Builder $q): Builder => $q->whereNull('tenant_id')->orWhere('tenant_id', $tenant->id))
              ->orderBy('name')->orderBy('id')
              ->lockForUpdate()
              ->firstOrFail();
          foreach ($users as $user) {
              $user->assignRole($admin);
              $this->line("  Assigned admin role to: {$user->email}");
          }
      });
      ```
      The explicit `setPermissionsTeamId($tenant->id)` the command already performs at `:91` **stays exactly where it is**.

      **Out of scope, deliberately and by name:** serialising the `DROP SCHEMA … CASCADE` itself. The lock cannot usefully span it — between the drop and the recreate the `roles` table does not exist, so no role-grant writer can be contending for anything the lock names, and holding one transaction across `DROP SCHEMA`, `CREATE SCHEMA` and a full migration run would block every other role writer in that tenant for the whole duration and would wrap DDL the migrator already transactions. This plan does **not** claim the reset is concurrency-safe as a whole; it claims only, and checkably, that the **role assignment** at the end can no longer interleave with another role-grant writer.

### Steps — part C: the tests

- [ ] **Step 7 — `PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest`, at CALL-SITE / METHOD level** (plan gate r1 **BLOCKER B0b-3**).

      **What was wrong with rev 1, stated so the replacement is not read as a refactor.** Rev 1 enumerated **files** by raw substring, and asserted "this file mentions `PermissionWriteLock` somewhere". Three consequences, each of which defeats the test's whole purpose:
      1. a **new unlocked method** added to `RoleController` or `UserController` passes, because the file still mentions the lock elsewhere;
      2. the row-order assertion accepts **one** correctly ordered query anywhere in the file, not one per writer;
      3. `str_contains` matches inside comments and docblocks — so wave 1's `TemplateDeltaApplier`, whose docblock says it does **not** take the lock and relies on its callers, would be marked LOCKED by its own disclaimer (wave-1 plan gate r1 M1-3). A proof that a comment can satisfy is not a proof.

      **The replacement enumerates CALLS and attributes each to the METHOD that contains it,** using `nikic/php-parser`, which is already a direct dev dependency (`apps/api/composer.json` `require-dev` `nikic/php-parser: ^5.7`, vendored at `apps/api/vendor/nikic/php-parser`) and is therefore available to the test suite with no new package. An AST walk cannot match a comment or a string literal, so no narrowing heuristic is needed and none is written — which also removes rev 1's "resolve the false matches honestly" prose, the place a future agent would have been tempted to silence a real writer.

      **Measured now, at `dev` `630afa86f`, by exactly the walk below: twenty writer methods.** Nine are runtime, eleven are initialization. After `lane/w-lot-a-1a` merges, `LotActionPermissionDelta`'s three writer methods join them and the seeder's two move to their lane line numbers; Phase 0's re-derivation is where that is captured. `RoleController::destroy` does **not** appear — `$role->delete()` writes neither `role_has_permissions` nor `model_has_roles` directly, it cascades — and it is nevertheless in `LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS` below, because the cascade deletes pivot rows and must be serialized against a concurrent grant. **A method that must hold the lock but is not itself a writer is declared, not discovered.**

      **The third category the ruling's two-way split needs.** `LotActionPermissionDelta` takes the lock in `execute()` and then writes from two **private helpers** — `provisionGeneralManager()` (`lane/w-lot-a-1a:…/LotActionPermissionDelta.php:154,157`) and `synchronizeSeededRole()` (`:175,177,180`) — while `execute()` itself acquires at `:54` and writes at `:83`. A flat "every writer method acquires the lock in its own body" rule would fail both helpers **falsely**. `LOCK_INHERITED_FROM` resolves that mechanically rather than by waiver: a helper may inherit its entry point's lock **only if** the test proves, from the same AST, that (a) the declared entry point acquires before it calls the helper, and (b) **no other method in the file calls that helper**. An inheriting helper that acquires a caller you did not declare fails.

      First the walker, as its own support class so wave 1's `PermissionSyncService` and `TemplateDeltaApplier` inherit it rather than re-implement it:

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Architecture\Support;

use PhpParser\Node;
use PhpParser\NodeTraverser;
use PhpParser\NodeVisitorAbstract;
use PhpParser\ParserFactory;
use RuntimeException;
use SplFileInfo;
use Symfony\Component\Finder\Finder;

/**
 * Enumerate, per METHOD, every call that writes `role_has_permissions` or
 * `model_has_roles`, plus every call that acquires the permission write lock —
 * from the AST, so a comment, a docblock or a string literal can never satisfy
 * either question (spec 4.3.2; plan gate r1 B0b-3).
 *
 * The unit of the census is `<relative path>::<method>`. A file is not a unit:
 * a file that mentions the lock once tells you nothing about the method added to
 * it last week. An anonymous class — every tenant migration is one — is keyed by
 * its path, which is unique and stable.
 */
final class PermissionWriterCensus
{
    /**
     * Instance-call names that write a grant or a role assignment. Spatie's
     * whole write surface for these two tables.
     */
    public const WRITER_METHODS = [
        'syncPermissions', 'givePermissionTo', 'revokePermissionTo',
        'assignRole', 'syncRoles', 'removeRole',
    ];

    /**
     * Static calls that create a catalogue row. Matched on the LAST name
     * segment, so both `Role::create(...)` and
     * `\Spatie\Permission\Models\Role::create(...)` are one match — and an
     * unrelated `Product::firstOrCreate(...)` is not a match at all, which is
     * what a raw `firstOrCreate` grep could not express.
     *
     * @var list<array{0: string, 1: string}>
     */
    public const WRITER_STATICS = [
        ['Role', 'create'], ['Role', 'firstOrCreate'],
        ['Permission', 'create'], ['Permission', 'firstOrCreate'],
    ];

    /**
     * Acquiring the tenant advisory lock. Three spellings, all real, none of
     * them a comment:
     *   - `$this->permissionWriteLock->acquire(...)`   the injected helper;
     *   - `PermissionWriteLock::acquire(...)`          a static call, if one
     *                                                  is ever added;
     *   - `$this->acquireTenantLock(...)`              lane/w-lot-a-1a's own
     *                                                  private helper, which
     *                                                  takes the SAME key and
     *                                                  the SAME hash function
     *                                                  (LotActionPermissionDelta
     *                                                  :131-135) — adopting the
     *                                                  key is what makes the two
     *                                                  writers one serialization
     *                                                  domain.
     */
    public const ACQUIRE_METHODS = ['acquire', 'acquireTenantLock'];

    /**
     * @param  list<string>  $roots  absolute directories to scan
     * @return array<string, array{writes: list<array{name: string, line: int}>, acquires: list<int>, calls: list<array{name: string, line: int}>}>
     */
    public function collect(array $roots): array
    {
        $parser = (new ParserFactory)->createForNewestSupportedVersion();
        $units = [];

        foreach ((new Finder)->files()->name('*.php')->in($roots) as $file) {
            /** @var SplFileInfo $file */
            $source = (string) $file->getContents();
            $ast = $parser->parse($source);
            if ($ast === null) {
                throw new RuntimeException('unparseable: '.$file->getPathname());
            }

            $relative = str_replace(base_path().'/', '', $file->getPathname());
            $visitor = new PermissionWriterVisitor($relative);
            $traverser = new NodeTraverser;
            $traverser->addVisitor($visitor);
            $traverser->traverse($ast);

            foreach ($visitor->units as $key => $unit) {
                $units[$key] = $unit;
            }
        }

        ksort($units);

        return $units;
    }

    /** @return list<string> the `<path>::<method>` keys that contain at least one write */
    public function writerMethods(array $units): array
    {
        $keys = [];
        foreach ($units as $key => $unit) {
            if ($unit['writes'] !== []) {
                $keys[] = $key;
            }
        }
        sort($keys);

        return $keys;
    }
}

/** @internal */
final class PermissionWriterVisitor extends NodeVisitorAbstract
{
    /** @var array<string, array{writes: list<array{name: string, line: int}>, acquires: list<int>, calls: list<array{name: string, line: int}>}> */
    public array $units = [];

    /** @var list<string> */
    private array $methodStack = [];

    public function __construct(private readonly string $relativePath) {}

    public function enterNode(Node $node): ?Node
    {
        if ($node instanceof Node\Stmt\ClassMethod) {
            $this->methodStack[] = $node->name->toString();
            $this->unit();

            return null;
        }

        if ($node instanceof Node\Expr\MethodCall && $node->name instanceof Node\Identifier) {
            $name = $node->name->toString();
            if (in_array($name, PermissionWriterCensus::WRITER_METHODS, true)) {
                $this->record('writes', $name, $node->getStartLine());
            }
            if (in_array($name, PermissionWriterCensus::ACQUIRE_METHODS, true)) {
                $this->record('acquires', $name, $node->getStartLine());
            }
            $this->record('calls', $name, $node->getStartLine());

            return null;
        }

        if ($node instanceof Node\Expr\StaticCall
            && $node->name instanceof Node\Identifier
            && $node->class instanceof Node\Name) {
            $class = $node->class->getLast();
            $method = $node->name->toString();
            foreach (PermissionWriterCensus::WRITER_STATICS as [$c, $m]) {
                if ($class === $c && $method === $m) {
                    $this->record('writes', $class.'::'.$method, $node->getStartLine());
                }
            }
            if ($class === 'PermissionWriteLock' && $method === 'acquire') {
                $this->record('acquires', $method, $node->getStartLine());
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    public function leaveNode(Node $node): ?Node
    {
        if ($node instanceof Node\Stmt\ClassMethod) {
            array_pop($this->methodStack);
        }

        return null;
    }

    private function key(): ?string
    {
        $method = end($this->methodStack);

        return $method === false ? null : $this->relativePath.'::'.$method;
    }

    private function unit(): void
    {
        $key = $this->key();
        if ($key !== null && ! isset($this->units[$key])) {
            $this->units[$key] = ['writes' => [], 'acquires' => [], 'calls' => []];
        }
    }

    private function record(string $bucket, string $name, int $line): void
    {
        $key = $this->key();
        if ($key === null) {
            return;
        }
        $this->unit();
        $this->units[$key][$bucket][] = $bucket === 'acquires' ? $line : ['name' => $name, 'line' => $line];
    }
}
~~~

      Then the test:

~~~php
<?php

declare(strict_types=1);

namespace Tests\Feature\Identity;

use PHPUnit\Framework\Attributes\Test;
use Tests\Architecture\Support\PermissionWriterCensus;
use Tests\TestCase;

/**
 * LOCKED ∪ LOCK_INHERITED_FROM ∪ INITIALIZATION_ONLY = the AST-enumerated set of
 * role-grant writer METHODS, and the three classes are pairwise DISJOINT
 * (spec 4.3.2, as corrected by plan gate r1 B0b-3).
 *
 * A writer METHOD added later belongs to none of the three and fails the build
 * with its path, its method name and the line of its first write — whether it is
 * the twenty-first or the fiftieth, and whether or not the file it lives in
 * already mentions the lock somewhere else.
 */
final class PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest extends TestCase
{
    /**
     * Runtime writer methods: reachable from an authenticated HTTP request, or
     * from a console command an operator runs against a live tenant that already
     * has operators in it. Each must call an acquire BEFORE its first write.
     *
     * RoleController::destroy carries no Spatie write call — `$role->delete()`
     * cascades — and is nevertheless declared, because the cascade deletes
     * role_has_permissions rows and must be serialized against a concurrent
     * grant. That is why this list is DECLARED rather than derived: it is a
     * superset of the enumerated writers by design, and
     * every_declared_entry_point_acquires_before_its_first_write covers both.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    private const LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS = [
        'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::store',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::update',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::destroy',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::assignRole',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::removeRole',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/UserController.php::store',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/UserController.php::update',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/EnsurePermissionsRunner.php::run',
        'app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Commands/ResetTenantCommand.php::handle',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/LotActionPermissionDelta.php::execute',
    ];

    /**
     * Private helpers that WRITE but do not acquire, because their single entry
     * point already holds the lock. NOT a waiver: the test proves, from the same
     * AST, that the entry point acquires before it calls the helper AND that no
     * other method calls the helper. Both of these are lane/w-lot-a-1a's.
     *
     * @var array<string, string>  helper => the entry point whose lock it inherits
     */
    private const LOCK_INHERITED_FROM = [
        'app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/LotActionPermissionDelta.php::provisionGeneralManager'
            => 'app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/LotActionPermissionDelta.php::execute',
        'app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/LotActionPermissionDelta.php::synchronizeSeededRole'
            => 'app/Modules/Identity/Application/Services/LotActionPermissionDelta.php::execute',
    ];

    /**
     * Provisioning, demo-seeder, legacy-seeder and once-per-database migration
     * writer METHODS. CLOSED and SHRINK-ONLY: a new entry needs an owner ruling,
     * and the set is pinned by EXACT equality so the exemption can only shrink.
     *
     * ResetTenantCommand::handle is NOT here — spec 4.3.2's rev-8 M-2 ruling
     * moved it to LOCKED, and this fixture shrank by exactly one row.
     *
     * PermissionSeeder::run IS here, and leaves in Task 10 of this wave, which
     * deletes the file. That is the fixture's second shrink and the only change
     * Task 10 makes to this test.
     *
     * @var list<string>
     */
    private const INITIALIZATION_ONLY = [
        'app/Modules/Tenant/Application/Services/TenantInitializationService.php::assignDefaultRoles',
        'database/seeders/DatabaseSeeder.php::createUsers',
        'database/seeders/DemoTenantSeeder.php::assignAdminRoleAndMembership',
        'database/seeders/DemoTenantSeeder.php::ensureTechnicianUser',
        'database/seeders/CoffeeShopSeeder.php::createTestUsers',
        'database/seeders/ParapharmacySeeder.php::createTestUsers',
        'database/seeders/DemoPharmacySeeder.php::seedTunisiaCashiers',
        'database/seeders/DemoPharmacySeeder.php::seedRoleCoverageUsers',
        'database/seeders/PermissionSeeder.php::run',
        'database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php::createPermissionsFrom',
        'database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php::createLegacyRoles',
        'database/migrations/tenant/2025_12_13_000001_ensure_settings_permissions.php::up',
        'database/migrations/tenant/2026_07_16_100100_register_manage_location_access_permission.php::up',
    ];

    /** @var array<string, array{writes: list<array{name: string, line: int}>, acquires: list<int>, calls: list<array{name: string, line: int}>}> */
    private array $units;

    protected function setUp(): void
    {
        parent::setUp();
        $this->units = (new PermissionWriterCensus)->collect([base_path('app'), base_path('database')]);
    }

    #[Test]
    public function the_three_classes_partition_the_enumerated_writer_methods(): void
    {
        $enumerated = (new PermissionWriterCensus)->writerMethods($this->units);
        $declared = [
            ...self::LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS,
            ...array_keys(self::LOCK_INHERITED_FROM),
            ...self::INITIALIZATION_ONLY,
        ];
        sort($declared);

        $unclassified = array_values(array_diff($enumerated, $declared));
        $this->assertSame(
            [],
            $unclassified,
            "these methods write a role grant and are in none of LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS, LOCK_INHERITED_FROM or "
            ."INITIALIZATION_ONLY:\n  ".implode("\n  ", array_map(
                fn (string $key): string => $key.'  ->  '.implode(', ', array_map(
                    static fn (array $w): string => $w['name'].'@'.$w['line'],
                    $this->units[$key]['writes'],
                )),
                $unclassified,
            )),
        );

        // The other direction: a declared entry that is no longer a writer is a
        // stale exemption, and a stale exemption is how a fixture stops shrinking.
        // LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS is deliberately a superset (see its docblock), so
        // only the two exemption lists are pinned in this direction.
        $exempt = [...array_keys(self::LOCK_INHERITED_FROM), ...self::INITIALIZATION_ONLY];
        sort($exempt);
        $this->assertSame(
            [],
            array_values(array_diff($exempt, $enumerated)),
            'an exempted method that no longer writes must be REMOVED from its fixture, not carried.',
        );
    }

    #[Test]
    public function the_three_classes_are_pairwise_disjoint(): void
    {
        $locked = self::LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS;
        $inherited = array_keys(self::LOCK_INHERITED_FROM);

        $this->assertSame([], array_values(array_intersect($locked, $inherited)));
        $this->assertSame([], array_values(array_intersect($locked, self::INITIALIZATION_ONLY)));
        $this->assertSame([], array_values(array_intersect($inherited, self::INITIALIZATION_ONLY)));
    }

    /**
     * THE assertion. Not "the file mentions the lock" — "THIS METHOD acquires,
     * and acquires BEFORE its first write".
     */
    #[Test]
    public function every_declared_entry_point_acquires_before_its_first_write(): void
    {
        foreach (self::LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS as $key) {
            $this->assertArrayHasKey($key, $this->units, "{$key} does not exist; the fixture is stale.");
            $unit = $this->units[$key];

            $this->assertNotSame(
                [],
                $unit['acquires'],
                "{$key} is a locked writer and never calls acquire(). A file-level mention elsewhere is not a lock: "
                .'an equality check that is not serialized against the operator edit path is not a guard.',
            );

            if ($unit['writes'] === []) {
                continue; // declared for its cascade (RoleController::destroy), nothing to order against
            }

            $firstAcquire = min($unit['acquires']);
            $firstWrite = min(array_column($unit['writes'], 'line'));
            $this->assertLessThan(
                $firstWrite,
                $firstAcquire,
                "{$key} writes at line {$firstWrite} but acquires at line {$firstAcquire}. Obligation (ii): the lock is "
                .'the FIRST statement in the transaction, and a decision read after a write it did not serialize is not a decision.',
            );
        }
    }

    #[Test]
    public function an_inheriting_helper_has_exactly_one_caller_and_that_caller_locks_first(): void
    {
        foreach (self::LOCK_INHERITED_FROM as $helperKey => $entryKey) {
            $this->assertArrayHasKey($helperKey, $this->units, "{$helperKey} does not exist; the fixture is stale.");
            $this->assertArrayHasKey($entryKey, $this->units);

            [$path, $helper] = explode('::', $helperKey, 2);

            // (a) the declared entry point acquires BEFORE it calls the helper.
            $entry = $this->units[$entryKey];
            $callLines = array_column(array_filter(
                $entry['calls'],
                static fn (array $c): bool => $c['name'] === $helper,
            ), 'line');
            $this->assertNotSame([], $callLines, "{$entryKey} does not call {$helper}(); the inheritance claim is false.");
            $this->assertNotSame([], $entry['acquires'], "{$entryKey} does not acquire.");
            $this->assertLessThan(min($callLines), min($entry['acquires']), "{$entryKey} calls {$helper}() before acquiring.");

            // (b) NOBODY ELSE in that file calls it. An inheriting helper with a
            // second, unlocked caller inherits nothing.
            foreach ($this->units as $key => $unit) {
                if ($key === $entryKey || ! str_starts_with($key, $path.'::')) {
                    continue;
                }
                $this->assertSame(
                    [],
                    array_values(array_filter($unit['calls'], static fn (array $c): bool => $c['name'] === $helper)),
                    "{$key} also calls {$helper}(), so {$helperKey} cannot inherit {$entryKey}'s lock. Either lock this "
                    .'caller too, or make the helper acquire for itself.',
                );
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * The named create-path property. RoleController::store is NOT exempt from
     * the lock; it is exempt from obligations (iii) and (iv), because the row it
     * locks does not exist yet.
     */
    #[Test]
    public function the_create_path_takes_the_lock_and_does_not_pretend_to_lock_a_row_it_is_about_to_insert(): void
    {
        $key = 'app/Modules/Identity/Presentation/Controllers/RoleController.php::store';
        $unit = $this->units[$key];

        $this->assertNotSame([], $unit['acquires'], 'store() must take the lock.');
        $this->assertContains('Role::create', array_column($unit['writes'], 'name'));
        $this->assertSame(
            [],
            array_values(array_filter($unit['calls'], static fn (array $c): bool => $c['name'] === 'lockForUpdate')),
            'a post-insert FOR UPDATE on the row this transaction just created buys nothing beyond the advisory lock it '
            .'already holds, and must not be added as ritual.',
        );
    }

    /**
     * Per WRITER METHOD, not per file: a method that takes FOR UPDATE takes it in
     * the lane's order. Rev 1 accepted one correctly ordered query anywhere in
     * the file, so a second, differently ordered one was invisible.
     */
    #[Test]
    public function every_row_locking_writer_method_uses_the_lane_row_order(): void
    {
        foreach ([...self::LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS, ...array_keys(self::LOCK_INHERITED_FROM)] as $key) {
            $unit = $this->units[$key] ?? null;
            if ($unit === null) {
                continue;
            }
            $names = array_column($unit['calls'], 'name');
            if (! in_array('lockForUpdate', $names, true)) {
                continue;
            }

            $orderBys = array_values(array_filter($unit['calls'], static fn (array $c): bool => $c['name'] === 'orderBy'));
            $this->assertGreaterThanOrEqual(
                2,
                count($orderBys),
                "{$key} takes FOR UPDATE with fewer than two orderBy() calls. The lane's order is roles.name ASC then "
                .'roles.id ASC, and a deterministic order is only a deadlock cure when every participant uses the SAME one.',
            );

            [$path, $method] = explode('::', $key, 2);
            $body = $this->methodSource($path, $method);
            $this->assertMatchesRegularExpression(
                "/orderBy\('name'\)\s*(->|\n\s*->)?orderBy\('id'\)/",
                $body,
                "{$key} orders its FOR UPDATE by something other than roles.name then roles.id.",
            );
        }
    }

    private function methodSource(string $relativePath, string $method): string
    {
        $source = (string) file_get_contents(base_path($relativePath));
        $start = (int) strpos($source, ' function '.$method.'(');
        $next = strpos($source, "\n    ", $start + 1);

        return substr($source, $start, $next === false ? strlen($source) : max(1, $next - $start) + 4000);
    }
}
~~~

> **The census is exact, not approximate — that is what the AST buys.** Run at `dev` `630afa86f`, the walk above returns **twenty** writer methods and **no** false positives: no comment, no docblock, no unrelated `Product::firstOrCreate`. Rev 1's own note — *"`enumerateWriters()` will report files this plan has not classified… resolve it by narrowing the enumeration honestly"* — is **withdrawn**, because there is nothing to narrow and therefore nothing an agent under time pressure could quietly narrow away. **If the first run reports an unclassified method, it is a real writer.** Classify it into `LOCKED_ENTRY_POINTS` (and wire the lock) or, only with an owner ruling, into `INITIALIZATION_ONLY`. Record the enumerated set verbatim in the handback.
>
> **After the lane merges, three methods join and none leave:** `LotActionPermissionDelta.php::execute`, `::provisionGeneralManager`, `::synchronizeSeededRole` — already in the fixtures above. Phase 0 re-derives their lines; the fixtures are keyed by **method name**, not by line, so a lane rebase cannot invalidate them.

      ```
      Phase 0.2.4: Add permissions:ensure(-fleet) and wire the write lock into every runtime role-grant writer

      One commit by spec ruling (§8, 0b-14). The 0b-window argument is the reason:
      permissions:ensure's only guard is an equality check, wave 1's customised_at
      column does not exist yet, and an equality check that is not serialized
      against the operator edit path is a race with no detector. Wiring the ten
      runtime writer entry points and shipping the command together means every
      interleaving is
      one of two — the operator commits before ensure takes the lock (the post-lock
      re-read sees the change, matchesPreWave0b is false, the role falls into
      admin_only=), or after it releases (the edit lands on top of a grant set ensure
      had already approved). There is no third, because no unlocked writer is left.

      ensure is create-only, grants admin unconditionally, grants a --grant-to role
      only when matchesPreWave0b() is true from the POST-LOCK read, snapshots
      eligibility ONCE per role and writes the approved additions as one batch,
      writes no marker, and takes the lane's team boundary and NULL-or-tenant role
      predicate verbatim — after the lane merges the legacy roles are NULL-team while
      general_manager is tenant-team-scoped, so a default-context lookup would lock
      one topology and write another.

      ResetTenantCommand moves from INITIALIZATION-ONLY to LOCKED (spec 4.3.2's
      rev-8 M-2 ruling): it refuses only in production, --force skips the prompt, and
      it recreates a live schema with no maintenance mode, so "no operator can be
      concurrent" was false for that row. The DROP SCHEMA itself stays out of scope,
      by name.

      A tenant with no resolvable `admin` is FAILED with reason=admin_role_missing
      and exits non-zero on the APPLYING and the DRY-RUN path. Its routes are gated
      on keys nobody holds, Spatie fails closed, and every caller including the
      operator gets 403 — so a run that cannot reach admin must never let a deploy
      checklist record permissions_ensure=ok.

      PermissionWriteLockCoverageTest is a CALL-SITE census over nikic/php-parser,
      not a file-substring grep: it attributes every Spatie write to the METHOD that
      contains it and asserts that method acquires BEFORE its first write. A new
      unlocked method in a file that mentions the lock elsewhere now fails, and a
      docblock can no longer satisfy the proof. Private helpers that write under a
      caller's lock are declared in LOCK_INHERITED_FROM and PROVEN — the entry point
      acquires before calling them, and nothing else calls them.

      Spec 4.3.5, 4.3.2. EC-20b, EC-20c. Plan gate r1 B0b-3, B0b-5, M0b-1, M0b-3.

      Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>
      Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_01Bp1WuD39YqaMWiHR8vPDNN
      ```

## Task 5 (0b-1 + 0b-7): declare `credit-notes.cancel` and fix the test that masks it — **ONE commit**

**Today's defect.** `POST /credit-notes/{creditNote}/cancel` already carries `->middleware('can:credit-notes.cancel')` (`apps/api/app/Modules/Document/Presentation/routes.php:269-271`) and **no tenant has ever had the row**. Spatie 6.25 swallows `PermissionDoesNotExist` in `checkPermissionTo`, so **every caller including `admin` gets a silent 403**. The key survives only in the legacy `PermissionSeeder.php:72` — evidence it was lost in a seeder consolidation, which is why §4.9.3 restores it *before* Task 11 deletes that file.

**Why 0b-7 rides in this commit.** `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/RefundResidualTenantIsolationTest.php:135-142` currently does `Permission::firstOrCreate(['name' => 'credit-notes.cancel', …])` and grants it, with a comment mischaracterising a permission that **does not exist in the catalogue** as one *"admin doesn't include by default"*. Replacing the `firstOrCreate` with a plain lookup reds the suite until the key is declared — and a knowingly red commit is not independently mergeable. Landing both together means the suite is green on **every** merged commit while still proving the key exists.

**Files**

- Modify: `apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php` — `legacyPermissionNames()` and `legacyRolePermissionGrants()`
- Modify: `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/RefundResidualTenantIsolationTest.php:135-142`
- Create: `apps/api/tests/Feature/Document/CreditNoteCancelPermissionTest.php`

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — declare the key.** In `legacyPermissionNames()`, beside its siblings (`credit-notes.view`, `.create`, `.post`), add `'credit-notes.cancel'`. In `legacyRolePermissionGrants()`, add it to the `manager` block on the line that already carries `'credit-notes.view', 'credit-notes.create', 'credit-notes.post'` (lane `:679`). **Do not touch `permissionNames()`, `rolePermissionGrants()`, `run()` or the marker branch** — `general_manager` inherits `manager`'s set through the lane's own `rolePermissionGrants()` derivation (lane `:101-102`), so declaring it once on `manager` gives it to both, exactly as `LegacyRoleBaseline::WAVE_0B_ADDITIONS` expects.

- [ ] **Step 2 — fix the masking test.**

~~~php
        // The two keys these routes require MUST already exist in the seeded
        // catalogue. A firstOrCreate here would mask a missing key: Spatie 6.25
        // swallows PermissionDoesNotExist in checkPermissionTo, so an undeclared
        // key silently 403s EVERY caller including admin — which is exactly what
        // `credit-notes.cancel` did until wave 0b declared it (spec 4.9.3).
        foreach (['credit-notes.cancel', 'invoices.cancel'] as $perm) {
            $permission = Permission::query()
                ->where('name', $perm)
                ->where('guard_name', 'sanctum')
                ->first();
            $this->assertNotNull(
                $permission,
                "permission '{$perm}' is not in the seeded catalogue — the route that requires it 403s every caller, admin included.",
            );
            $this->userA->givePermissionTo($permission);
        }
~~~

      `invoices.cancel` is already in the catalogue (`apps/api/database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php:176` at HEAD; lane `:256`), so it passes unchanged — this loop now proves both rather than creating both.

- [ ] **Step 3 — the route test.** `CreditNoteCancelPermissionTest`: a holder of `credit-notes.cancel` reaches `POST /api/v1/credit-notes/{creditNote}/cancel` (assert the **row**: the credit note's status, not just a 200), a non-holder gets 403 with the standard envelope, and — the regression pin — a freshly seeded tenant's `permissions` table **contains** the key.

- [ ] **Step 4 — nothing in `Wave0bAdditionConstantTest` is affected, and nothing is left red.** That file's cases are pure constant arithmetic and were green from Task 1 (plan gate r1 **B0b-2**; rev 1 carried a knowingly-red catalogue assertion through Tasks 1–6 here). The catalogue-facing property — *every one of the nineteen is declared in the seeder* — ships as `Wave0bAdditionCatalogueTest` in **Task 6 Step 4**, the commit that declares the last of them. This task declares one of the nineteen and leaves the suite green.

      cd /Users/houssamr/Projects/syneriva/apps/erp/.worktrees/rbac-w0b/apps/api && ./vendor/bin/phpstan analyse --level=8 database/seeders/RolesAndPermissionsSeeder.php tests/Feature/Document/CreditNoteCancelPermissionTest.php

## Task 6 (0b-2/0b-3/0b-4): declare the remaining eighteen keys and gate their routes

**The nineteen keys, authoritative.** Seven go to `manager` (and therefore to `general_manager`, which derives from it); twelve are `admin`-only. See **Q-0b-2** and **Q-0b-3** for the two places the spec did not name the verbs and what this plan chose.

| # | Key | Template defaults | Routes it gates |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `credit-notes.cancel` | `manager` (Task 5) | `POST /credit-notes/{creditNote}/cancel` (gate already present) |
| 2 | `services.view` | `manager` | `GET /services`, `GET /services/{service}` |
| 3 | `services.create` | `manager` | `POST /services` |
| 4 | `services.update` | `manager` | `PATCH /services/{service}`, `DELETE /services/{service}` (Q-0b-2) |
| 5 | `service-categories.view` | `manager` | `GET /service-categories`, `/tree`, `/{category}` |
| 6 | `service-categories.create` | `manager` | `POST /service-categories` |
| 7 | `service-categories.update` | `manager` | `PATCH /service-categories/{category}`, `DELETE /service-categories/{category}` |
| 8 | `channels.view` | *admin only* | `GET /channels`, `GET /channels/orders`, `GET /channels/{id}/sync-operations`, `GET /channels/{id}/orders` |
| 9 | `channels.create` | *admin only* | `POST /channels` |
| 10 | `channels.update` | *admin only* | `POST /channels/{id}/test-connection`, `POST /channels/{id}/resync` |
| 11 | `channels.operate` | *admin only* | `POST /channels/{channelId}/orders/{orderId}/promote`, `.../products/{productId}/publish`, `.../unpublish` |
| 12 | `categories.view` | *admin only* | `GET /categories`, `/tree`, `/{id}` |
| 13 | `categories.create` | *admin only* | `POST /categories` |
| 14 | `categories.update` | *admin only* | `PUT /categories/{id}`, `POST /categories/reorder` |
| 15 | `categories.delete` | *admin only* | `DELETE /categories/{id}` |
| 16 | `progression.view` | *admin only* | `GET progression/profile`, `/milestones`, `/modules`, `/recommendations` |
| 17 | `progression.manage` | *admin only* | `POST progression/register`, `/modules/{moduleId}/activate`, `/recommendations/{id}/accept`, `/dismiss` |
| 18 | `purchase-hub.orders.create` | *admin only* | `POST purchase-hub/orders` |
| 19 | `companies.create` | *admin only* | `POST /companies` |

> **`POST /api/v1/companies` is the sharpest one.** Today `CreateCompanyRequest::authorize()` returns `true` and the controller then self-grants `MembershipRole::Owner` to the caller (`apps/api/app/Modules/Company/Presentation/Controllers/CompanyController.php:148-155`), so **any** authenticated user in **any** of the seeded roles can create a company and become its owner. It gains `can:companies.create`, `admin`-only.
>
> **The Channel webhook ingress group is NOT touched.** `Route::prefix('api/v1/webhooks/channels')` is deliberately unauthenticated — external platforms call it and it is protected by `Str::isUuid()` plus `throttle:channel-webhook`. It is in the **public** allow-list, not the uncovered count. Adding `can:` there would break every channel integration.
>
> **`progression.manage` and the `manage` invariant.** §4.2.2's rule is *"a resource may declare `Manage` **or** any of `Create`/`Update`/`Delete`, not both"*. `progression.*` declares `view` + `manage` and no CRUD verb, so it satisfies the rule as written. Wave 1's `PermissionRegistryConsistencyTest::manage_is_never_mixed_with_crud_on_one_resource` must stay green on it — check that when writing the `ProgressionPermissionManifest`.

### The frontend half of this task, which rev 1 did not have (plan gate r1 **BLOCKER B0b-4**)

Rev 1's Step 3 changed **one** FE guard — `services.edit` → `services.update` — and nothing else, while the task adds admin-only gates to Channels, Categories, Progression and company creation. Every one of those has a live caller that a non-admin can reach today, so shipping the backend half alone means a manager's page fires a request the code already knows will be refused. The plan even *accepted* that (*"Categories create is 403 with the standard envelope"*), which contradicts this plan's own reviewer gate — *"a fifth caller anywhere in `apps/web/src` or `apps/pos/src` is a blocker… a page that fires a request it knows will be refused is the defect this task exists to close"*. **Rev 2 guards every affected caller.**

**The census, measured at `dev` `630afa86f` and re-measured against `lane/w-lot-a-1a` `52f5ad796`.** The lane touches `apps/web/src/routes/index.tsx` (its batches block only) and two batch pages; it touches **none** of the channels, categories, progression, services, company or CompanySelector files below. **0b runs after that lane merges**, so the `routes/index.tsx` numbers an executing agent will see are the **post-merge** ones — the lane inserts a `WLOTA1A_WEB_FINGERPRINT` export at `:349-350` (**+2** for every line after `:347`) and widens the batches block around `:1200-1255` (**+7** for every line after it). Both columns are given; the content in every audited region is byte-identical across the two refs (verified by `diff <(sed -n '560,1199p' dev) <(sed -n '562,1201p' lane)`).

| # | Caller | `dev` | post-merge | Guard today | 0b adds |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---|---|

**Three keys need no frontend work, and saying so is part of the census:**
- **`channels.create` / `channels.update` / `channels.operate`** — `createChannel`, `testConnection`, `resyncChannel` and `promoteChannelOrder` are defined in `features/channels/api.ts:20,24,28,41` and have **zero callers** in `apps/web/src`; `ChannelCreateWizard.tsx:49-51`'s "Test connection" button has **no `onClick`**. Only `GET /channels` and `GET /channels/orders` are live, which is why only `channels.view` appears in the table.
- **`purchase-hub.orders.create`** — `grep -rn "purchase-hub" apps/web/src apps/pos/src` returns **nothing** in either app.
- **`credit-notes.cancel`** — no caller either: `apps/web/src` posts to `/credit-notes` (create, `features/documents/CreateCreditNotePage.tsx:236`) but never to `/credit-notes/{id}/cancel`.

Each of those three is recorded in the handback as *gated, no live caller* — a fact the reviewer needs, and the reason the wave gates them anyway is that the route exists and the authority question is real.

### The `/service-categories` versus `/services/categories` mismatch — RULED

**The backend route is canonical. The frontend calls are wrong and are fixed here.** `apps/api/app/Modules/Service/Presentation/routes.php:38,41,44,47,50,53` defines `service-categories{,/tree,/{category}}` under the `api/v1` prefix at `:20`; **no route named `services/categories` exists**. Six production call sites in `apps/web/src` target the non-existent path:

| File:line | Call | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:58` | `api.get('/services/categories')` | `'/service-categories'` |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:66` | `api.post('/services/categories', data)` | `'/service-categories'` |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:79` | ``api.put(`/services/categories/${id}`, data)`` | ``api.patch(`/service-categories/${id}`, data)`` — the backend defines **PATCH** at `routes.php:50`, not PUT |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:92` | ``api.delete(`/services/categories/${id}`)`` | ``/service-categories/${id}`` |
| `apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceForm.tsx:72` | `api.get('/services/categories')` | `'/service-categories'` |
| **`apps/web/src/features/services/ServiceListPage.tsx:49`** | `api.get('/services/categories')` | `'/service-categories'` — **a third file**; the gate register named only the first two, and fixing two of three would leave the service list broken |

Plus the test doubles that assert the wrong URL: `apps/web/src/features/services/__tests__/tenantScope.test.tsx:100,195,219,234,309,316,335,342,390`.

**Two things that are NOT bugs and must not be "fixed":** `ServiceListPage.tsx:117`'s `to="/services/categories"` and `Sidebar.tsx:148`'s `href: '/services/categories'` are **SPA route paths**, not API calls — they stay exactly as they are. And the React-Query keys already read `tenantScopedKey(['service-categories'])` (`ServiceForm.tsx:70`, `ServiceListPage.tsx:47`, `ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:56`, `_invalidation.ts:5,23`) — **unchanged**, so CLAUDE.md rule 14's TanStack audit stays green.

> **Why this rides in this task rather than a ticket.** This task's own verification is a probe that service-category CRUD works for a `manager`. That probe cannot pass while every call goes to a route that does not exist, and the failure it would produce (404) is indistinguishable at a glance from the 403 this wave is testing for. Fixing the path is a precondition for having any evidence at all. **It is also, by itself, a user-visible repair**, so the Playwright spec below covers it.

**Files:** the seeder's two legacy methods, seven route files, sixteen frontend callers, three service-category call sites, the label bundles, and one new Playwright spec.

**Steps**

- [ ] **Step 1 — declare all eighteen keys in `legacyPermissionNames()`,** grouped by module with a one-line comment naming the wave, and give `manager` the six `services.*`/`service-categories.*` keys in `legacyRolePermissionGrants()`. Twelve keys appear in **no** role block — `admin` receives them through `'admin' => self::legacyPermissionNames()` (lane `:662`) and through `ensure`'s unconditional admin grant.

- [ ] **Step 2 — gate the routes, file by file,** adding `->middleware('can:<key>')` to each route in the table above. Rules for every edit:
      - the gate goes on the **route**, never in the controller (E-2);
      - the group's existing middleware array is untouched, including `module:Workshop` / `module:Ecommerce` / `module:Inventory`;
      - `->name(...)` stays where it is;
      - one route per line-change, so the diff is readable and the reviewer can check the key against the table.

- [ ] **Step 3 — the frontend guards: all sixteen callers from the census above, plus the three service-category call sites.** This is the half rev 1 did not have (plan gate r1 **B0b-4**). Work in the census table's order and do not stop at the route guards — a route guard stops a *navigation*, but every one of these pages also fires a query on mount, and a query with a stale `enabled` is a 403 the router never sees.

      **(a) The route guards.** `RequirePermission` **ANDs** `moduleKey` and `permission` (`apps/web/src/features/auth/components/RequirePermission.tsx:48-58`), so each is a one-attribute addition beside the existing module key. Post-merge lines in brackets:
      ```tsx
      // channels index [:2255], channels/new [:2265], :id/products [:2275],
      // :id/sync [:2285], :id/orders [:2295], ecommerce/orders [:2309]
      <RequirePermission moduleKey="inventory" permission="channels.view">
      // categories [:1194]
      <RequirePermission moduleKey="inventory" permission="categories.view">
      // services/:id/edit [:1627] — the D5 canonical spelling of the key
      // this wave declares. :1584/[:1591]'s services.create is already canonical.
      <RequirePermission permission="services.update">
      ```
      **`growth` and `growth/modules` [`:3210`, `:3219`] have no wrapper at all today** — they are bare `<SuspenseWrapper>` children. Wrap each in `<RequirePermission permission="progression.view">`, matching the shape of the routes around them. **`/company-onboarding` [`:554`]** keeps `<RequireAuth>` and gains `<RequirePermission permission="companies.create">` **inside** it — the order matters, because an unauthenticated visitor must reach the login redirect, not a permission-denied surface.

      **(b) The query guards.** In each hook or page, take `const { hasPermission } = usePermissions()` and add the flag to the existing `enabled`:
      ```tsx
      // ChannelListPage.tsx:24
      enabled: Boolean(tenantId && companyId) && hasPermission('channels.view'),
      // ChannelCreateWizard.tsx:13-16 — it has NO enabled option today, so add one
      enabled: hasPermission('channels.view'),
      // useAggregateChannelOrders.ts:22
      enabled: tenantId !== null && companyId !== null && hasPermission('channels.view'),
      // useCategories.ts:114 (the tree query CategoriesPage.tsx:27 consumes)
      enabled: !!tenantId && !!companyId && hasPermission('categories.view'),
      // useCompanyProgression.ts:89 and :104, useModuleReadiness.ts:25,
      // useRecommendations.ts:24
      enabled: !!tenantId && !!companyId && hasPermission('progression.view'),
      ```
      > **`useCategories.ts` is a shared hook — check its other consumers before editing it.** If `useCategoryTree()` is used by a surface that is not the Categories page, gate the **page's** call rather than the hook, so the addition does not silently disable an unrelated screen. Name whichever you chose in the handback.

      **(c) The action controls.** `CategoryTreeView` renders each row control purely on prop presence (`components/CategoryTreeView.tsx:84,93,102` — `{onAddSubcategory && (…)}`, `{onEdit && (…)}`, `{onDelete && (…)}`), which is the lever: pass `undefined` instead of a handler and the control disappears with no new markup and no new colour literal.
      ```tsx
      // CategoriesPage.tsx:183-188
      <CategoryTreeView
        onAddSubcategory={hasPermission('categories.create') ? handleAddSub : undefined}
        onEdit={hasPermission('categories.update') ? handleEdit : undefined}
        onDelete={hasPermission('categories.delete') ? handleDelete : undefined}
      />
      ```
      The two "New category" buttons at `:140-146` and `:172-178` render inside `{hasPermission('categories.create') && (…)}`. For progression, `ModuleCard.tsx:27` and `RecommendationCard.tsx:21-22` gate their activate / accept / dismiss controls on **`progression.manage`**. For company creation, `CompanySelector.tsx:113-120`'s "Add company" button and `AddCompanyModal.tsx`'s trigger both render only for **`companies.create`** — note that the CompanySelector button sits **outside** the `hasMultipleCompanies &&` block that ends at `:112`, so it is currently shown to every user and the new condition must wrap the button itself.

      > **`apps/web/src/hooks/usePermissions.ts` is expected UNTOUCHED.** Every one of the six new keys used above (`channels.view`, `categories.view/create/update/delete`, `progression.view/manage`, `companies.create`, `services.update`) reaches `hasPermission()` through the **generated** map, which Task 14 Step 3 regenerates. `MODULE_PERMISSIONS` is only for `moduleKey=`, and this task adds no new module key. If a guard turns out to need one, that is a finding — record it, because `usePermissions.ts` is in `lane/w-lot-a-1a`'s diff and the overlap must be re-checked at that moment.

      **(d) The `/service-categories` path fix**, per the ruling above: `ServiceCategoryListPage.tsx:58,66,79,92`, `ServiceForm.tsx:72`, `ServiceListPage.tsx:49`, and the nine test-double URLs in `__tests__/tenantScope.test.tsx`. `:79` also changes `api.put` → `api.patch`. **Do not touch** `ServiceListPage.tsx:117` or `Sidebar.tsx:148` (SPA paths) or any `tenantScopedKey(['service-categories'])` (already correct).

      **(e) Vitest, red first.** For each of Channels, Categories, Progression and company-create, add a **pair** of cases to the owning test file, in the shape 0b-15 already uses (Task 13b Step 2): *a caller without the key issues **no** request*, and *a caller with it issues exactly one*. The zero-versus-one is the evidence; capture the red verbatim.
